Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime

Statements of Mathlib (commit c31114ff81), each with its Lean source, its translation to Dedukti by Export.lean, and its informalization by Informath, in English, as a formula, and in French, Czech, German and Swedish, which the buttons at each entry show there, and the check boxes above the entries everywhere. The Dedukti translation drops implicit and instance arguments and maps Lean's arithmetic and logic to Informath's base (mapping.tsv); the other Mathlib constants are worded by the symbol table mathlib.dkgf, whose English wordings Informath also renders in the other languages. Constants without a wording appear as $\mathrm{name}$.

Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime.Defs source

Nat.PrimedefDefs.lean:42
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[pp_nodot, wikidata Q49008, oeis A000040]
def Prime (p : ℕ) :=
  Irreducible p
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__Prime : (p : Elem Nat) -> Prop :=
  p => Irreducible p.
English
Let $p$ be a natural number. Then $p$ is prime, if $p$ is irreducible.
French
Soit $p$ un nombre naturel. Alors $p$ est premier si $p$ est irréductible.
Czech
Nechť $p$ je přirozené číslo. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, jestliže $p$ je ireducibilní.
German
Sei $p$ eine natürliche Zahl. Dann ist $p$ prim, wenn $p$ irreduzibl ist.
Swedish
Låt $p$ vara ett naturligt tal. Då är $p$ primt, om $p$ är irreducibelt.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \operatorname{Prime} (p):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{Irreducible} (p)$

theorem irreducible_iff_nat_prime (a : ℕ) : Irreducible a ↔ Nat.Prime a :=
  Iff.rfl
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__irreducible_iff_nat_prime : (a : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (Irreducible a) (prime a)) :=
  a => Iff__rfl (Irreducible a).
English
Let $a$ be a natural number. Then $a$ is irreducible, if and only if $a$ is prime.
French
Soit $a$ un nombre naturel. Alors $a$ est irréductible si et seulement si $a$ est premier.
Czech
Nechť $a$ je přirozené číslo. Tedy $a$ je ireducibilní, právě tehdy, když $a$ je prvočíselné.
German
Sei $a$ eine natürliche Zahl. Dann ist $a$ irreduzibl, genau dann, wenn $a$ prim ist.
Swedish
Låt $a$ vara ett naturligt tal. Då är $a$ irreducibelt, om och endast om $a$ är primt.
Formula
$a \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{Irreducible} (a)\leftrightarrow \mathrm{prime} (a)$
Proof

Nat.Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:88
Lean
theorem Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd {p : ℕ} (pp : p.Prime) (m : ℕ) (hm : m ∣ p) :
    m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
  have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
  rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
  apply Or.imp_right _ this
  rintro rfl
  rw [hn, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__Prime__eq_one_or_self_of_dvd : (p : Elem Nat) -> (pp : Proof (prime p)) -> (m : Elem Nat) -> (hm : Proof (divide m p)) -> Proof (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)).
English
Let $p$ and $m$ be natural numbers such that $p$ is prime and $m$ divides $p$. Then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soient $p$ et $m$ des nombres naturels tels que $p$ soit premier et $m$ divise $p$. Alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p$ a $m$ je přirozené číslo takové, že $p$ je prvočíselné a $m$ dělí $p$. Tedy $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
German
Seien $p$ und $m$ natürliche Zahlen derart, dass $p$ prim ist und $m$ $p$ teilt. Dann $m = 1$ oder $m = p$.
Swedish
Låt $p$ och $m$ vara naturliga tal sådana att $p$ är primt och $m$ delar $p$. Då $m = 1$ eller $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural , \mathrm{prime} (p), m \in \Natural , \divides {m}{p}\vdash m = 1 \vee m = p$
Proof
6 lines
1.Let $n$ be an element of $\Natural$ such that $p = m \times n$.obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
2.$m$ is a unit or $n$ is a unit.have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
3.$m = 1$ or $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.It is enough to show that if $n = 1$, then $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.Assume that $n = 1$.rintro rfl
6.$m = p$.rw [hn, mul_one]
Proof, French

Nat.prime_deftheoremDefs.lean:98
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[inherit_doc Nat.Prime]
theorem prime_def {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, m ∣ p → m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1
  refine ⟨mt Nat.isUnit_iff.mp h1.ne', ?_⟩
  rintro a b rfl
  simp only [Nat.isUnit_iff]
  refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
  rw [← mul_right_inj' (Nat.ne_zero_of_lt h1), ← hab, ← hab, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__prime_def : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m => if (divide m p) (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)))))).
English
Let $p$ be a natural number. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soit $p$ un nombre naturel. Alors $p$ est premier si et seulement si $2 \leq p$ et pour tous des nombres naturels $m$, si $m$ divise $p$, alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p$ je přirozené číslo. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $m$, jestliže $m$ dělí $p$, pak $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
German
Sei $p$ eine natürliche Zahl. Dann ist $p$ prim, genau dann, wenn $2 \leq p$ und für alle natürliche Zahlen $m$, wenn $m$ $p$ teilt, dann $m = 1$ oder $m = p$.
Swedish
Låt $p$ vara ett naturligt tal. Då är $p$ primt, om och endast om $2 \leq p$ och för alla naturliga tal $m$, om $m$ delar $p$, så $m = 1$ eller $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge (\forall m \in \Natural . \divides {m}{p}\rightarrow m = 1 \vee m = p)$
Proof
9 lines
1.Assume that $2 \leq p$ and for all elements $m$ of $\Natural$, if $\divides {m}{p}$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
2.It is enough to show that $p$ is prime.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
3.$1 < p$.have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1

Nat.prime_def_lttheoremDefs.lean:107
Lean
theorem prime_def_lt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m < p, m ∣ p → m = 1 :=
  prime_def.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun _ =>
        ⟨fun h l d => (h d).resolve_right (ne_of_lt l), fun h d =>
          (le_of_dvd (le_of_succ_le p2) d).lt_or_eq_dec.imp_left fun l => h l d⟩

English
Let $p$ be a natural number. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < p$, then if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$.
French
Soit $p$ un nombre naturel. Alors $p$ est premier si et seulement si $2 \leq p$ et pour tous des nombres naturels $m$, si $m < p$, alors si $m$ divise $p$, alors $m = 1$.
Czech
Nechť $p$ je přirozené číslo. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $m$, jestliže $m < p$, pak jestliže $m$ dělí $p$, pak $m = 1$.
German
Sei $p$ eine natürliche Zahl. Dann ist $p$ prim, genau dann, wenn $2 \leq p$ und für alle natürliche Zahlen $m$, wenn $m < p$, dann wenn $m$ $p$ teilt, dann $m = 1$.
Swedish
Låt $p$ vara ett naturligt tal. Då är $p$ primt, om och endast om $2 \leq p$ och för alla naturliga tal $m$, om $m < p$, så om $m$ delar $p$, så $m = 1$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge (\forall m \in \Natural . m < p \rightarrow \divides {m}{p}\rightarrow m = 1)$

Nat.prime_def_lt'theoremDefs.lean:114

theorem prime_def_lt' {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m < p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun m =>
        ⟨fun h m2 l d => not_lt_of_ge m2 ((h l d).symm ▸ by decide), fun h l d => by
          rcases m with (_ | _ | m)
          · omega
          · rfl
          · exact (h (le_add_left 2 m) l).elim d⟩

theorem prime_def_le_sqrt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m ≤ sqrt p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt'.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      ⟨fun a m m2 l => a m m2 <| lt_of_le_of_lt l <| sqrt_lt_self p2, fun a m m2 l mdvd@⟨k, e⟩ => by
        rcases le_sqrt_of_eq_mul e with hm | hk
        · exact a m m2 hm mdvd
        · rw [mul_comm] at e
          exact a k (Nat.lt_of_mul_lt_mul_right (a := m) (by rwa [one_mul, ← e])) hk ⟨m, e⟩⟩

Nat.prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eqtheoremDefs.lean:133

theorem prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eq {p : ℕ} :
    p.Prime ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ¬ ∃ m n, m < p ∧ n < p ∧ m * n = p := by
  push Not
  simp_rw [prime_def_lt, dvd_def, exists_imp]
  refine and_congr_right fun hp ↦ forall_congr' fun m ↦ (forall_congr' fun h ↦ ?_).trans forall_comm
  simp_rw [Ne, forall_comm (β := _ = _), eq_comm, imp_false, not_lt]
  refine forall₂_congr fun n hp ↦ ⟨by simp_all, fun hpn ↦ ?_⟩
  have := mul_ne_zero_iff.mp (hp ▸ show p ≠ 0 by lia)
  exact (Nat.mul_eq_right (by lia)).mp
    (hp.symm.trans (hpn.antisymm (hp ▸ Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left _ (by lia))))

Nat.prime_of_coprimetheoremDefs.lean:144

theorem prime_of_coprime (n : ℕ) (h1 : 1 < n) (h : ∀ m < n, m ≠ 0 → n.Coprime m) : Prime n := by
  refine prime_def_lt.mpr ⟨h1, fun m mlt mdvd => ?_⟩
  have hm : m ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    rw [zero_dvd_iff] at mdvd
    exact mlt.ne' mdvd
  exact (h m mlt hm).symm.eq_one_of_dvd mdvd
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__prime_of_coprime : (n : Elem Nat) -> (h1 : Proof (Lt 1 n)) -> (h : Proof (forall Nat (m => if (Lt m n) (if (Neq m 0) (Nat__Coprime n m))))) -> Proof (prime n).
English
Let $n$ be a natural number such that $1 < n$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < n$, then if $m \neq 0$, then $n$ and $m$ are coprime. Then $n$ is prime.

theorem dvd_prime_two_le {p m : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) (H : 2 ≤ m) : m ∣ p ↔ m = p :=
  (dvd_prime pp).trans <| or_iff_right_of_imp <| Not.elim <| ne_of_gt H

Nat.minFacAuxdefDefs.lean:207
If n < k * k, then minFacAux n k = n, if k | n, then minFacAux n k = k. Otherwise, minFacAux n k = minFacAux n (k+2) using well-founded recursion. If n is odd and 1 < n, then minFacAux n 3 is the smallest prime factor of n.
This definition is by well-founded recursion, so rfl or decide cannot be used. One can use norm_num to prove Nat.prime n for small n.

def minFacAux (n : ℕ) : ℕ → ℕ

theorem minFac_le_of_dvd {n : ℕ} : ∀ {m : ℕ}, 2 ≤ m → m ∣ n → minFac n ≤ m := by
  by_cases n1 : n = 1
  · exact fun m2 _ => n1.symm ▸ le_trans (by simp) m2
  · apply (minFac_has_prop n1).2.2

theorem le_minFac {m n : ℕ} : n = 1 ∨ m ≤ minFac n ↔ ∀ p, Prime p → p ∣ n → m ≤ p :=
  ⟨fun h p pp d =>
    h.elim (by rintro rfl; cases pp.not_dvd_one d) fun h =>
      le_trans h <| minFac_le_of_dvd pp.two_le d,
    fun H => or_iff_not_imp_left.2 fun n1 => H _ (minFac_prime n1) (minFac_dvd _)⟩

1 lines

Soit $n$ un nombre naturel tel que $2 \leq n$. Alors $n$ n'est pas premier si et seulement si le plus petit facteur premier de $n$ est inférieur à $n$.
Czech
Nechť $n$ je přirozené číslo takové, že $2 \leq n$. Tedy $n$ není prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když nejmenší prvočíselný faktor $n$ je menší než $n$.
German

Swedish

Nat.minFac_le_divtheoremDefs.lean:347
Lean
theorem minFac_le_div {n : ℕ} (pos : 0 < n) (np : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n :=
  match minFac_dvd n with
  | ⟨0, h0⟩ => absurd pos <| by rw [h0, mul_zero]; decide
  | ⟨1, h1⟩ => by
    rw [mul_one] at h1
    rw [prime_def_minFac, not_and_or, ← h1, eq_self_iff_true, _root_.not_true, _root_.or_false,
      not_le] at np
    rw [le_antisymm (le_of_lt_succ np) (succ_le_of_lt pos), minFac_one, Nat.div_one]
  | ⟨x + 2, hx⟩ => by
    conv_rhs =>
      congr
      rw [hx]
    rw [Nat.mul_div_cancel_left _ (minFac_pos _)]
    exact minFac_le_of_dvd (le_add_left 2 x) ⟨minFac n, by rwa [mul_comm]⟩

1 lines

Nat.minFac_sq_le_selftheoremDefs.lean:364
The square of the smallest prime factor of a composite number n is at most n.
Lean
theorem minFac_sq_le_self {n : ℕ} (w : 0 < n) (h : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ^ 2 ≤ n :=
  have t : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n := minFac_le_div w h
  calc
    minFac n ^ 2 = minFac n * minFac n := sq (minFac n)
    _ ≤ n / minFac n * minFac n := Nat.mul_le_mul_right (minFac n) t
    _ ≤ n := div_mul_le_self n (minFac n)
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__minFac_sq_le_self : (n : Elem Nat) -> (w : Proof (Lt 0 n)) -> (h : Proof (not (prime n))) -> Proof (Leq (pow (Nat__minFac n) 2) n) :=
  n => w => h => Trans__trans (Trans__trans (sq Nat (Nat__minFac n)) (Nat__mul_le_mul_right (Nat__minFac n) (div n (Nat__minFac n)) (Nat__minFac n) (Nat__minFac_le_div n w h))) (Nat__div_mul_le_self n (Nat__minFac n)).

Nat.minFac_eq_one_ifftheoremDefs.lean:372
Lean
@[simp]
theorem minFac_eq_one_iff {n : ℕ} : minFac n = 1 ↔ n = 1 := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    by_contra hn
    have := minFac_prime hn
    rw [h] at this
    exact not_prime_one this
  · rintro rfl
    simp [minFac, minFacAux]

Nat.minFac_eq_two_ifftheoremDefs.lean:383

@[simp]
theorem minFac_eq_two_iff (n : ℕ) : minFac n = 2 ↔ 2 ∣ n := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    rw [← h]
    exact minFac_dvd n
  · intro h
    have ub := minFac_le_of_dvd (le_refl 2) h
    have lb := minFac_pos n
    refine ub.eq_or_lt.resolve_right fun h' => ?_
    suffices n.minFac = 1 by simp_all
    exact (le_antisymm (Nat.succ_le_of_lt lb) (Nat.lt_succ_iff.mp h')).symm

Nat.coprime_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:406
Lean
theorem coprime_of_dvd {m n : ℕ} (H : ∀ k, Prime k → k ∣ m → ¬k ∣ n) : Coprime m n := by
  rw [coprime_iff_gcd_eq_one]
  by_contra g2
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hpdvd⟩ := exists_prime_and_dvd g2
  apply H p hp <;> apply dvd_trans hpdvd
  · exact gcd_dvd_left _ _
  · exact gcd_dvd_right _ _
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__coprime_of_dvd : (m : Elem Nat) -> (n : Elem Nat) -> (H : Proof (forall Nat (k => if (prime k) (if (divide k m) (not (divide k n)))))) -> Proof (Nat__Coprime m n).
English
Let $m$ and $n$ be natural numbers such that for all natural numbers $k$, if $k$ is prime, then if $k$ divides $m$, then $k$ does not divide $n$. Then $m$ and $n$ are coprime.
French
Soient $m$ et $n$ des nombres naturels tels que pour tous les nombres naturels $k$, si $k$ est premier, alors si $k$ divise $m$, alors $k$ ne divise pas $n$. Alors $\NatCoprimeMACRoC {m}{n}$.
Czech
Nechť $m$ a $n$ je přirozené číslo takové, že pro všechna přirozená čísla $k$, jestliže $k$ je prvočíselné, pak jestliže $k$ dělí $m$, pak $k$ nedělí $n$. Tedy $m$ a $n$ je nesoudělné.
German
Seien $m$ und $n$ natürliche Zahlen derart, dass für alle natürliche Zahlen $k$, wenn $k$ prim ist, dann wenn $k$ $m$ teilt, dann $k$ nicht $n$ teilt. Dann $\NatCoprimeMACRoC {m}{n}$.
Swedish
Låt $m$ och $n$ vara naturliga tal sådana att för alla naturliga tal $k$, om $k$ är primt, så om $k$ delar $m$, så delar $k$ inte $n$. Då $\NatCoprimeMACRoC {m}{n}$.
Formula
$m , n \in \Natural , \forall k \in \Natural . \mathrm{prime} (k)\rightarrow \divides {k}{m}\rightarrow \neg (\divides {k}{n})\vdash \NatCoprimeMACRoD {m}{n}$
Proof
7 lines
1.It is enough to show that the greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ is equal to $1$.rw [coprime_iff_gcd_eq_one]
2.Assume that the greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ is not equal to $1$.by_contra g2
3.It is enough to show that we have a contradiction.by_contra g2
4.Let $p$ be an element of $\Natural$ such that $p$ is prime and $p$ divides the greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$.obtain ⟨p, hp, hpdvd⟩ := exists_prime_and_dvd g2
5.It is enough to show that the greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $m$ and the greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $n$.apply H p hp <;> apply dvd_trans hpdvd
6.The greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $m$.exact gcd_dvd_left _ _
7.The greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $n$.exact gcd_dvd_right _ _

  ⟨fun h => ⟨h.ne_zero, h.not_isUnit, fun _ _ => h.dvd_mul.mp⟩, Prime.irreducible⟩

def Nat__prime_iff : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (Prime p)) :=

Nat.forall_prime_iff_two_and_oddtheoremBasic.lean:110
A property holds for every prime iff it holds for 2 and for every odd prime.
Lean
theorem forall_prime_iff_two_and_odd {P : ℕ → Prop} :
    (∀ p, p.Prime → P p) ↔ P 2 ∧ ∀ p, p.Prime → Odd p → P p :=
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__forall_prime_iff_two_and_odd : (P : (Elem Nat -> Prop)) -> Proof (iff (forall Nat (p => if (prime p) (P p))) (and (P 2) (forall Nat (p1 => if (prime p1) (if (odd p1) (P p1)))))).
English
Let $P$ be a function from natural numbers to propositions. Then for all natural numbers $p$, if $p$ is prime, then $P$ holds for $p$, if and only if $P$ holds for $2$ and for all natural numbers $p_{1}$, if $p_{1}$ is prime, then if $p_{1}$ is odd, then $P$ holds for $p_{1}$.
French
Soit $P$ une fonction des nombres naturels à des propositions. Alors pour tous les nombres naturels $p$, si $p$ est premier, alors $P$ tient pour $p$ si et seulement si $P$ tient pour $2$ et pour tous des nombres naturels $p_{1}$, si $p_{1}$ est premier, alors si $p_{1}$ est impair, alors $P$ tient pour $p_{1}$.
Czech
Nechť $P$ je funkce z přirozených čísl do výroků. Tedy pro všechna přirozená čísla $p$, jestliže $p$ je prvočíselné, pak $P$ platí pro $p$, právě tehdy, když $P$ platí pro $2$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $p_{1}$, jestliže $p_{1}$ je prvočíselné, pak jestliže $p_{1}$ je liché, pak $P$ platí pro $p_{1}$.
German
Sei $P$ eine Funktion aus natürlichen Zahlen nach Propositionen. Dann für alle natürliche Zahlen $p$, wenn $p$ prim ist, dann hält $P$ für $p$, genau dann, wenn $P$ für $2$ hält und für alle natürliche Zahlen $p_{1}$, wenn $p_{1}$ prim ist, dann wenn $p_{1}$ ungerade ist, dann $P$ für $p_{1}$ hält.
Swedish
Låt $P$ vara en funktion från naturliga tal till påståenden. Då för alla naturliga tal $p$, om $p$ är primt, så håller $P$ för $p$, om och endast om $P$ håller för $2$ och för alla naturliga tal $p_{1}$, om $p_{1}$ är primt, så om $p_{1}$ är udda, så håller $P$ för $p_{1}$.
Formula
$P \in \Natural \rightarrow \mathrm{Prop} \vdash (\forall p \in \Natural . \mathrm{prime} (p)\rightarrow P (p))\leftrightarrow P (2)\wedge (\forall p_{1} \in \Natural . \mathrm{prime} (p_{1})\rightarrow \mathrm{odd} (p_{1})\rightarrow P (p_{1}))$
Nat.forall_prime_of_two_of_oddtheoremBasic.lean:118
To prove a property of all primes, it is enough to prove it for 2 and for the odd primes.
Lean
alias ⟨_, forall_prime_of_two_of_odd⟩ := forall_prime_iff_two_and_odd
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__forall_prime_of_two_of_odd : (P : (Elem Nat -> Prop)) -> (h : Proof (and (P 2) (forall Nat (p1 => if (prime p1) (if (odd p1) (P p1)))))) -> (p : Elem Nat) -> (h1 : Proof (prime p)) -> Proof (P p) :=
  P => iffEr (forall Nat (p => if (prime p) (P p))) (and (P 2) (forall Nat (p1 => if (prime p1) (if (odd p1) (P p1))))) (Nat__forall_prime_iff_two_and_odd P).
English
Let $P$ be a function from natural numbers to propositions such that $P$ holds for $2$ and for all natural numbers $p_{1}$, if $p_{1}$ is prime, then if $p_{1}$ is odd, then $P$ holds for $p_{1}$. Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Then $P$ holds for $p$.
French

Czech

theorem Prime.not_dvd_mul {p m n : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) (Hm : ¬p ∣ m) (Hn : ¬p ∣ n) : ¬p ∣ m * n :=
  mt pp.dvd_mul.1 <| by simp [Hm, Hn]

theorem coprime_primes {p q : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) (pq : Prime q) : Coprime p q ↔ p ≠ q :=
  pp.coprime_iff_not_dvd.trans <| not_congr <| dvd_prime_two_le pq pp.two_le
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__coprime_primes : (p : Elem Nat) -> (q : Elem Nat) -> (pp : Proof (prime p)) -> (pq : Proof (prime q)) -> Proof (iff (Nat__Coprime p q) (Neq p q)) :=

Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime.Infinite source

Nat.exists_infinite_primestheoremInfinite.lean:36
Euclid's theorem on the *infinitude of primes*. Here given in the form: for every n, there exists a prime number p ≥ n.
Lean
theorem exists_infinite_primes (n : ℕ) : ∃ p, n ≤ p ∧ Prime p :=
  let p := minFac (n ! + 1)
  have f1 : n ! + 1 ≠ 1 := ne_of_gt <| succ_lt_succ <| factorial_pos _
  have pp : Prime p := minFac_prime f1
  have np : n ≤ p :=
    le_of_not_ge fun h =>
      have h₁ : p ∣ n ! := dvd_factorial (minFac_pos _) h
      have h₂ : p ∣ 1 := (Nat.dvd_add_iff_right h₁).2 (minFac_dvd _)
      pp.not_dvd_one h₂
  ⟨p, np, pp⟩
